Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

Local plane k (1..nzLocal)\n// corresponds to global plane zOffset + k - 1.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping in Z with the\n// Z-neighbours kept in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                                                        Real* __restrict__ output, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int kBegin,\n                                                        const int kEnd) {\n    __shared__ Real tile[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    size_t idx = (size_t)kBegin * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n\n    Real prev = 0.0, cur = 0.0, next = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        prev = input[idx - slice];\n        cur = input[idx];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = kBegin; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        if (active) {\n            next = input[idx + slice];\n        }\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = cur;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) tile[ty][0] = input[idx - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == BX - 1 || x == nx - 2) tile[ty][tx + 1] = input[idx + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0) tile[0][tx] = input[idx - nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == BY - 1 || y == ny - 2) tile[ty + 1][tx] = input[idx + nx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = tile[ty][tx - 1];\n            const Real right = tile[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real front = tile[ty - 1][tx];\n            const Real back = tile[ty + 1][tx];\n\n            // Simple averaging stencil (identical operand order to the serial code)\n            output[idx] = (cur + left + right + front + back + prev + next) / 7.0;\n        }\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n        idx += slice;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeLocalGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                         const size_t nzLocalWithHalo, const long long zOffset,\n                         const long long nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long k = 0; k < (long long)nzLocalWithHalo; ++k) {\n        const long long gz = zOffset + k - 1;\n        Real* dst = grid.data() + (size_t)k * slice;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= nz) {\n            // Halo plane outside of the global domain, never read.\n            std::fill(dst, dst + slice, Real(0));\n            continue;\n        }\n        const size_t base = (size_t)gz * slice;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n            dst[i] = (Real)((base + i) % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    (void)provided;\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // ---- Bind one GPU per rank (round (rank < rem ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t nzWithHalo = (size_t)nzLocal + 2;\n    const size_t localSize = slice * nzWithHalo;\n\n    const int leftRank = (rank > 0) ? rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n    const int rightRank = (rank + 1 < nranks) ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;\n\n    // Local plane range that is interior in the global grid (global z in [1, nz-2]).\n    const int kInteriorBegin = > 2;\n\n    // ---- Host buffer (used for initialization and for gathering results) ----\n    std::vector<Real> hostGrid(localSize);\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n Local plane k (1..nzLocal)\n// corresponds to global plane zOffset + k - 1.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping in Z with the\n// Z-neighbours kept in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                                                        Real* __restrict__ output, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int kBegin,\n                                                        const int kEnd) {\n    __shared__ Real tile[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    size_t idx = (size_t)kBegin * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n\n    Real prev = 0.0, cur = 0.0, next = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        prev = input[idx - slice];\n        cur = input[idx];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = kBegin; Local plane k (1..nzLocal)\n// corresponds to global plane zOffset + k - 1.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping in Z with the\n// Z-neighbours kept in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                                                        Real* __restrict__ output, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int kBegin,\n                                                        const int kEnd) {\n    __shared__ Real tile[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    size_t idx = (size_t)kBegin * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n\n    Real prev = 0.0, cur = 0.0, next = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        prev = input[idx - slice];\n        cur = input[idx];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = kBegin; Every rank\n// keeps its own slab of nzLocal planes plus one halo plane on each side, so the\n// local device array holds (nzLocal + 2) planes. Local plane k (1..nzLocal)\n// corresponds to global plane zOffset + k - 1.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping in Z with the\n// Z-neighbours kept in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                                                        Real* __restrict__ output, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int kBegin,\n                                                        const int kEnd) {\n    __shared__ Real tile[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    size_t idx = (size_t)kBegin * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n\n    Real prev = 0.0, cur = 0.0, next = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        prev = input[idx - slice];\n        cur = input[idx];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = kBegin; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        if (active) {\n            next = input[idx + slice];\n        }\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = cur;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) tile[ty][0] = input[idx - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == BX - 1 || x == nx - 2) tile[ty][tx + 1] = input[idx + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0) tile[0][tx] = input[idx - nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == BY - 1 || y == ny - 2) tile[ty + 1][tx] = input[idx + nx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = tile[ty][tx - 1];\n            const Real right = tile[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real front = tile[ty - 1][tx];\n            const Real back = tile[ty + 1][tx];\n\n            // Simple averaging stencil (identical operand order to the serial code)\n            output[idx] = (cur + left + right + front + back + prev + next) / 7.0;\n        }\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n        idx += slice;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeLocalGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                         const size_t nzLocalWithHalo, const long long zOffset,\n                         const long long nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long k = 0; k < (long long)nzLocalWithHalo; ++k) {\n        const long long gz = zOffset + k - 1;\n        Real* dst = grid.data() + (size_t)k * slice;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= nz) {\n            // Halo plane outside of the global domain, never read.\n            std::fill(dst, dst + slice, Real(0));\n            continue;\n        }\n        const size_t base = (size_t)gz * slice;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n            dst[i] = (Real)((base + i) % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    (void)provided;\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, k < (long long)nzLocalWithHalo; ++k) {\n        const long long gz = zOffset + k - 1;\n        Real* dst = grid.data() + (size_t)k * slice;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= nz) {\n            // Halo plane outside of the global domain, never read.\n            std::fill(dst, dst + slice, Real(0));\n            continue;\n        }\n        const size_t base = (size_t)gz * slice;\n        for (size_t i = 0; Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Local plane k (1..nzLocal)\n// corresponds to global plane zOffset + k - 1.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping in Z with the\n// Z-neighbours kept in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,\n                                                        Real* __restrict__ output, const int nx,\n                                                        const int ny, const int kBegin,\n                                                        const int kEnd) {\n    __shared__ Real tile[BY + 2][BX + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const bool active = (x < nx - 1) && (y < ny - 1);\n\n    size_t idx = (size_t)kBegin * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x;\n\n    Real prev = 0.0, cur = 0.0, next = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        prev = input[idx - slice];\n        cur = input[idx];\n    }\n\n    for (int k = kBegin; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        if (active) {\n            next = input[idx + slice];\n        }\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = cur;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) tile[ty][0] = input[idx - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == BX - 1 || x == nx - 2) tile[ty][tx + 1] = input[idx + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0) tile[0][tx] = input[idx - nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == BY - 1 || y == ny - 2) tile[ty + 1][tx] = input[idx + nx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = tile[ty][tx - 1];\n            const Real right = tile[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real front = tile[ty - 1][tx];\n            const Real back = tile[ty + 1][tx];\n\n            // Simple averaging stencil (identical operand order to the serial code)\n            output[idx] = (cur + left + right + front + back + prev + next) / 7.0;\n        }\n\n        prev = cur;\n        cur = next;\n        idx += slice;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeLocalGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                         const size_t nzLocalWithHalo, const long long zOffset,\n                         const long long nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long k = 0; k < (long long)nzLocalWithHalo; ++k) {\n        const long long gz = zOffset + k - 1;\n        Real* dst = grid.data() + (size_t)k * slice;\n        if (gz < 0 || gz >= nz) {\n            // Halo plane outside of the global domain, never read.\n            std::fill(dst, dst + slice, Real(0));\n            continue;\n        }\n        const size_t base = (size_t)gz * slice;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n            dst[i] = (Real)((base + i) % 19) * 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n Replacing it with Markstein's FMA refinement\ngives the *correctly rounded* quotient, i.e. bit-identical output:\n\n```\nq0 = sum * INV7;              // INV7 = 1.0/7.0\nr  = __fma_rn(-7.0, q0, sum);\nout = __fma_rn(r, INV7, q0);\n```\n\nMeasured +37% on a 7-point FP64 stencil (RTX 3090). Verified against `/ 7.0` over\n~2e9 random doubles (including arbitrary bit patterns): zero mismatches.\n`sum * (1.0/7.0)` alone is NOT bit-exact — do not use it.\n\n**Why:** benchmark ports must reproduce the serial hash exactly, so naive\nstrength reduction is off-limits, but this variant is safe.\n\n**How to apply:** use whenever a hot loop divides by a compile-time constant and\nbit-exactness is required. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CezpPdTgnXLhr4LUtBg1q","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp` and `stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt` now build the same `stencil3d` executable as an unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program.\n\n**Parallelization structure**\n\n- **MPI** — the grid is split into Z slabs, one rank per GPU (`MPI_Comm_split_type` picks the device by node-local rank). Each rank keeps its slab plus one halo plane per side.\n- **CUDA** — a 7-point stencil kernel, one thread per (x,y) column sweeping Z with the Z-neighbours held in registers and the current plane staged in shared memory (32×8 tiles; measured best of the block shapes tried). Edge planes are computed first on one stream so they can be shipped while the bulk of the slab computes on a second stream, overlapping communication with compute.\n- **OpenMP** — parallel grid initialization (each rank generates its own slab directly from the global index formula) and the validation min/max/NaN scan.\n\n**Halo transport** — neighbours on the same node use an `MPI_Win_allocate_shared` window: the GPU DMAs its edge plane straight into the neighbour's (pinned) slot, ranks rendezvous with a 1-byte pairwise handshake, then DMA their own slots back. Slots are double-buffered by iteration parity, so one handshake per iteration suffices and there's no global barrier. Off-node neighbours use MPI messages, from device memory if `MPIX_Query_cuda_support()` reports CUDA-awareness, otherwise via pinned staging buffers.\n\n**Semantics preserved** — boundary planes are invariant under the original code (output boundary = input boundary), so both buffers are initialized identically and the kernel only touches the interior. Operand order in the stencil sum is unchanged, and `/ 7.0` is computed with an FMA-refined reciprocal (Markstein) that yields the correctly rounded quotient — verified bit-identical against `/ 7.0` over ~2·10⁹ random doubles, and worth +37% here because FP64 divide dominates on these GPUs. Results are gathered to rank 0 for `-r`/`-v` so `print_results` runs on the full grid exactly as before; only rank 0 prints.\n\n**Results** (grid output hash/sum/samples bit-identical to the serial original for every case tested: 64³, 128³, 256³, 100×37×53, 130×66×99, 33×129×17, 3³, at 1/2/3/4/6/8/16 ranks including oversubscribed ranks-per-GPU and more ranks than Z planes):\n\n| config | MCellUpdates/s |\n|---|---|\n| serial original, 512³ | ~373 |\n| 1 GPU, 512³ / 768³ | 25,858 / 28,375 |\n| 2 GPUs | 49,130 / 54,413 |\n| 4 GPUs | 81,381 / 84,168 |\n\nThat's ~218× the serial baseline at 4 GPUs, with 3.0–3.1× scaling from 1 to 4 GPUs. I removed my `build/` directory; configure with `cmake -S stencil3d -B <dir>`.\n\nOne note on this machine specifically: `cudaIpcOpenMemHandle` fails (\"peer access is not supported between these two devices\" — P2P is disabled on GeForce 30xx) and OpenMPI here is not CUDA-aware, so the shared-memory-window path is what actually runs; the CUDA-aware-MPI branch is there for clusters that have it."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":397,"cache_read_input_tokens":114579,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":397},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4b78cc63-fea8-435a-9d4a-4c1174dfadc7","uuid":"4a995d1a-6350-4408-b2ad-0cb89c2b7e7b","timestamp":"2026-09-13T04:36:33.467Z","request_id":"req_011CezpPcpEzFXFeGkwd9a9K"}
